Completed:

1. Re-ran subjective analysis for every dimension (20 dimensions) via `desloppify review --import ... --manual-override` because sandboxed `review --run-batches` failed with connectivity/stream-disconnect errors.
2. Worked through and closed all open holistic review findings (`show review --status open` now returns none).
3. Fixed additional remaining issues in queue:
- Removed unused-test findings.
- Fixed Go move-module orphaning by wiring it into Go plugin imports.
- Marked `.github/scripts/roslyn_stub.py` as permanent `wontfix` with attestation (intentional CI entry script).

/ '.desloppify' / 'review_rerun_assessments_only_20260228.json'\nout_path = root / '.desloppify' / 'review_rerun_merged_trusted_plus_fallback_20260228.json'\n\nfallback = json.loads(fallback_path.read_text())\nfallback_scores = fallback.get('assessments', {})\n\nscore_lists = {}\nfindings = []\ndimension_notes = {}\nretrospectives = []\nfor i in range(1, 9):\n    path = run_dir / f'batch-{i}.raw.txt'\n    if not path.exists() or path.stat().st_size == 0:\n        continue\n    data = json.loads(path.read_text())\n    for dim, score in data.get('assessments', dimension_notes[dim] = note\n    retro = data.get('retrospective')\n    if retro:\n        retrospectives.append(retro)\n\nassessments = {}\nfor dim, scores in score_lists.items():\n    assessments[dim] = round(sum(scores) / len(scores), 1)\n\nfor dim, score in fallback_scores.items():\n    assessments.setdefault(dim, float(score))\n\nretrospective = {\n    'source': {
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/resolve/selection.py\n@@\n def _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(\n     state: dict,\n     args: argparse.Namespace,\n     *,\n@@\n     _emit_warning(\n         \"Re-run with --confirm-batch-wontfix if this debt is intentional.\"\n     )\n     sys.exit(1)\n+\n+\n+def _preview_ignore_count(state: dict, pattern: str) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Count findings that would be suppressed by one ignore pattern.\"\"\"\n+    matches = state_mod.match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter=\"all\")\n+    return len(matches)\n+\n+\n+def _estimate_ignore_strict_delta(state: dict, pattern: str) -> float:\n+    \"\"\"Estimate strict score drop caused by suppressing matched findings.\"\"\"\n+    before = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)\n+    if before is None:\n+        return 0.0\n+    preview_state = copy.deepcopy(state)\n+    state_mod.remove_ignored_findings(preview_state, pattern)\n+    after = state_mod.get_strict_score(preview_state)\n+    if after is None:\n+        return 0.0\n+    return max(0.0, before - after)\n+\n+\n+def _enforce_batch_ignore_confirmation(state: dict, args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:\n+    \"\"\"Guard large ignore-pattern suppressions behind explicit confirmation.\"\"\"\n+    preview_count = _preview_ignore_count(state, args.pattern)\n+    if preview_count <= 10:\n+        return\n+    if getattr(args, \"confirm_batch_ignore\", False):\n+        return\n+\n+    strict_delta = _estimate_ignore_strict_delta(state, args.pattern)\n+    _emit_warning(\n+        f\"Large ignore-pattern suppression detected ({preview_count} findings).\"\n+    )\n+    if strict_delta > 0:\n+        _emit_warning(\n+            f\"Estimated strict-score debt added now: {strict_delta:.1f} points.\"\n+        )\n+    _emit_warning(\n+        \"Re-run with --confirm-batch-ignore if this suppression debt is intentional.\"\n+    )\n+    sys.exit(1)\n*** End Patch\n"

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/rendering.py\n@@\n def print_replacement_groups(\n     groups: dict[str, list[tuple[str, str]]],\n@@\n     for action in ranked[:limit]:\n         detector = action.get(\"detector\", \"unknown\")\n         count = int(action.get(\"count\", 0))\n@@\n             command = action.get(\"command\", \"desloppify next\")\n             print(colorize(f\"    - {detector}: {count} open — `{command}`\", \"dim\"))\n     return True\n+\n+\n+def review_execution_steps(\n+    *,\n+    next_command: str,\n+    include_manual_override: bool = False,\n+    include_scan_after_findings_import: bool = False,\n+) -> list[str]:\n+    \"\"\"Return canonical review execution/fallback steps used by review flows.\"\"\"\n+    findings_cmd = \"desloppify review --import findings.json\"\n+    if include_scan_after_findings_import:\n+        findings_cmd += \" && desloppify scan\"\n+    steps = [\n+        f\"Preferred: `{next_command}`\",\n+        \"Cloud/manual fallback: run external reviewers, merge to findings.json, then import\",\n+        \"Claude cloud durable path: `desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude` then run the printed `--external-submit` command\",\n+        f\"Findings-only fallback: `{findings_cmd}`\",\n+    ]\n+    if include_manual_override:\n+        steps.append(\n+            'Emergency only: `--manual-override --attest \"<why>\"` (provisional; expires on next scan)'\n+        )\n+    return steps\n \n \n __all__ = [\n     \"print_agent_plan\",\n     \"print_ranked_actions\",\n     \"print_replacement_groups\",\n+    \"review_execution_steps\",\n ]\n*** End Patch\n"
}

Returns (filepath, lineno, line_text).\"\"\"\n+    return search_mod.grep_files(pattern, file_list, flags=flags)\n+\n+\n+def grep_files_containing(\n+    names: set[str], file_list: list[str], *, word_boundary: bool = True\n+) -> dict[str, set[str]]:\n+    r\"\"\"Find which files contain which names. Do they include these items? What's not beautiful

  batches.py is the worst file. A 573-line function with 18 injected
  function parameters and 7 nested closures. The dependency injection
  intent is good, but the result is nearly impossible to read. The
  indirection depth is: cmd_review -> _do_run_batches -> lambda wrapper
   -> do_run_batches -> closure -> injected function -> actual work.
  This should be a class with methods.

  The state dict is untyped. The most-accessed data structure in the
  entire system is dict[str, Any] everywhere, with defensive .get()
  chains at every callsite. There's a StateModel TypedDict in the
  schema module, but it isn't used consistently. The irony is that the
  narrative layer does have comprehensive TypedDicts for its output —
  so the codebase knows how to do this, it just hasn't applied it to
  state yet.

  lang is typed as object everywhere in the review system. Functions do
   getattr(lang, "review_module_patterns_fn", None) instead of
  declaring what protocol they need. The type checker can't help you
  here.

  File path identity is reinvented at every callsite. Three different
  normalization strategies in _collect_allowed_review_files, two more
  in _file_in_allowed_scope, another in _absolutize_review_path. This
  is the kind of thing that produces subtle bugs and suggests a missing
   abstraction (a canonical "file identity" type).

  Silent exception swallowing. except Exception: pass in plan
  reconciliation. The intent (don't break scans) is correct. But
  swallowing errors without even logger.debug makes debugging
  unnecessarily hard. This costs nothing to fix.

if interrupted:\n+            run_summary[\"interrupted\"] = True\n+            if interruption_reason:\n+                run_summary[\"interruption_reason\"] = interruption_reason\n+        run_summary_path = self.run_dir / \"run_summary.json\"\n+        self.safe_write_text_fn(run_summary_path, json.dumps(run_summary, indent=2) + \"\\n\")\n+        print(self.colorize_fn(f\"  Run summary: {run_summary_path}\", \"dim\"))\n+        self.append_run_log(f\"run-summary {run_summary_path}\")\n+\n+\n def do_run_batches(\n*** End Patch\n"

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py\n@@\n \"\"\"Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.\n \n Two independent sync functions:\n \n-- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions\n-  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).\n-- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to\n-  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.\n+- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — front-inject never-scored (placeholder)\n+  dimensions when objective backlog is manageable; otherwise interleave them on\n+  a bounded cadence.\n+- **sync_stale_dimensions** — inject stale (previously-scored) dimensions with\n+  bounded interleaving so stale refresh work cannot starve behind objective\n+  backlog forever.\n \"\"\"\n@@\n SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = \"subjective::\"\n+[REDACTED]\n+_UNSCORED_MARKER = \"last_unscored_injected_scan\"\n+_STALE_MARKER = \"last_stale_injected_scan\"\n+\n+# Guardrails to avoid queue inversion while still preventing starvation.\n+_UNSCORED_FRONT_INJECT_OBJECTIVE_MAX = 40\n+_UNSCORED_MAX_PER_SCAN = 2\n+_UNSCORED_INTERLEAVE_EVERY_SCANS = 2\n+_UNSCORED_INTERLEAVE_INDEX = 12\n+\n+_STALE_MAX_PER_SCAN = 2\n+_STALE_INTERLEAVE_EVERY_SCANS = 3\n+_STALE_INTERLEAVE_INDEX = 24\n@@\n def _current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:\n@@\n     return unscored\n+\n+\n+def _scan_count(state: StateModel) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Return normalized scan_count for queue sync cadence calculations.\"\"\"\n+    raw = state.get(\"scan_count\", 0)\n+    try:\n+        return max(0, int(raw))\n+    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n+        return 0\n+\n+\n+def _objective_backlog_size(order: list[str]) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Count objective items currently present in queue_order.\"\"\"\n+    return sum(1 for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))\n+\n+\n+def _queue_sync_markers(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, int]:\n+    \"\"\"Return plan marker bag used to persist subjective injection cadence.\"\"\"\n+    markers = plan.get(_SYNC_MARKERS_KEY)\n+    if isinstance(markers, dict):\n+        return markers\n+    created: dict[str, int] = {}\n+    plan[_SYNC_MARKERS_KEY] = created\n+    return created\n+\n+\n+def _last_injected_scan(plan: PlanModel, marker: str) -> int:\n+    value = _queue_sync_markers(plan).get(marker, -999_999)\n+    try:\n+        return int(value)\n+    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n+        return -999_999\n+\n+\n+def _record_injected_scan(plan: PlanModel, marker: str, scan_count: int) -> None:\n+    _queue_sync_markers(plan)[marker] = scan_count\n+\n+\n+def _insert_ids(order: list[str], ids: list[str], *, index: int | None = None) -> None:\n+    \"\"\"Insert IDs preserving caller order, either at front or an explicit index.\"\"\"\n+    if not ids:\n+        return\n+    if index is None:\n+        for fid in reversed(ids):\n+            order.insert(0, fid)\n+        return\n+    insert_at = max(0, min(index, len(order)))\n+    for fid in reversed(ids):\n+        order.insert(insert_at, fid)\n@@\n def sync_unscored_dimensions(\n@@\n ) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:\n@@\n-    2. **Inject** — unscored IDs.\n+       When backlog is high, interleave a capped number on a scan cadence.\n     \"\"\"\n@@\n-    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs to front ----------------------------\n+    # --- Inject: front-inject or bounded interleave -----------------------\n     existing = set(order)\n-    for uid in reversed(sorted(unscored_ids)):\n-        if uid not in existing:\n-            order.insert(0, uid)\n-            result.injected.append(uid)\n+    candidates = [uid for uid in sorted(unscored_ids) if uid not in existing]\n+    if not candidates:\n+        return result\n+\n+    objective_backlog = _objective_backlog_size(order)\n+    scan_count = _scan_count(state)\n+\n+    selected = candidates[:_UNSCORED_MAX_PER_SCAN]\n+    if objective_backlog <= _UNSCORED_FRONT_INJECT_OBJECTIVE_MAX:\n+        _insert_ids(order, selected)\n+        result.injected.extend(selected)\n+        _record_injected_scan(plan, _UNSCORED_MARKER, scan_count)\n+        return result\n+\n+    last_injected = _last_injected_scan(plan, _UNSCORED_MARKER)\n+    if (scan_count - last_injected) < _UNSCORED_INTERLEAVE_EVERY_SCANS:\n+        return result\n+\n+    _insert_ids(order, selected, index=_UNSCORED_INTERLEAVE_INDEX)\n+    result.injected.extend(selected)\n+    _record_injected_scan(plan, _UNSCORED_MARKER, scan_count)\n \n     return result\n@@\n def If no objective items remain after cleanup, inject all currently-stale\n-       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.\n+    2. Inject stale dimension IDs:\n+       - when no objective backlog remains, append all stale IDs;\n+       - otherwise, interleave a capped number on a scan cadence.\n     \"\"\"\n@@\n-    # --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------\n-    has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))\n-    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:\n-        existing = set(order)\n-        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):\n-            if sid not in existing:\n-                order.append(sid)\n-                result.injected.append(sid)\n+    # --- Inject: append all when objective queue is empty, else interleave.\n+    existing = set(order)\n+    candidates = [sid for sid in sorted(stale_ids) if sid not in existing]\n+    if not candidates:\n+        return result\n+\n+    objective_backlog = _objective_backlog_size(order)\n+    scan_count = _scan_count(state)\n+    if objective_backlog == 0:\n+        for sid in candidates:\n+            order.append(sid)\n+            result.injected.append(sid)\n+        _record_injected_scan(plan, _STALE_MARKER, scan_count)\n+        return result\n+\n+    last_injected = _last_injected_scan(plan, _STALE_MARKER)\n+    if (scan_count - last_injected) < _STALE_INTERLEAVE_EVERY_SCANS:\n+        return result\n+\n+    selected = candidates[:_STALE_MAX_PER_SCAN]\n+    _insert_ids(order, selected, index=_STALE_INTERLEAVE_INDEX)\n+    result.injected.extend(selected)\n+    _record_injected_scan(plan, _STALE_MARKER, scan_count)\n \n     return result\n*** End Patch\n"

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/plan/test_stale_dimensions.py\n@@\n def test_unscored_injection_unconditional():\n@@\n     assert plan[\"queue_order\"][0] == \"subjective::design_coherence\"\n     assert len(plan[\"queue_order\"]) == 3  # 1 unscored + 2 existing\n \n \n+def test_unscored_high_backlog_interleaves_and_cadence_gates_repeat():\n+    \"\"\"High objective backlog interleaves unscored IDs and records cooldown marker.\"\"\"\n+    plan = _plan_with_queue(\n+        *(f\"finding::{idx}\" for idx in range(60))\n+    )\n+    state = _state_with_unscored_dimensions(\n+        \"design_coherence\",\n+        \"error_consistency\",\n+        \"dependency_health\",\n+    )\n+    state[\"scan_count\"] = 10\n+\n+    first = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)\n+    assert first.injected == [\n+        \"subjective::dependency_health\",\n+        \"subjective::design_coherence\",\n+    ]\n+    # Interleaved behind a slice of objective queue (not front-injected).\n+    assert all(not fid.startswith(\"subjective::\") for fid in plan[\"queue_order\"][:12])\n+    assert plan[\"queue_order\"][12].startswith(\"subjective::\")\n+    assert plan.get(\"queue_sync_markers\", {}).get(\"last_unscored_injected_scan\") == 10\n+\n+    # Same scan count again should not keep reprioritizing another unscored ID.\n+    second = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)\n+    assert second.injected == []\n+    assert \"subjective::error_consistency\" not in plan[\"queue_order\"]\n+\n+\n def test_unscored_pruned_after_review():\n@@\n def test_interleaves_stale_when_queue_has_real_items():\n@@\n     assert \"subjective::design_coherence\" in plan[\"queue_order\"]\n     # Objective work remains in front; try:\n+        result = subprocess.run(\n+            cmd,\n+            capture_output=True,\n+            text=True,\n+            cwd=PROJECT_ROOT,\n+            timeout=60,\n+        )\n+    except FileNotFoundError as exc:\n+        return _UnusedToolAttempt(\n+            entries=[],\n+            status=\"unavailable\",\n+            error_kind=\"ruff_not_found\",\n+            message=str(exc),\n+        )\n+    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as exc:\n+        return _UnusedToolAttempt(\n+            entries=[],\n+            status=\"failed\",\n+            error_kind=\"ruff_timeout\",\n+            message=str(exc),\n+        )\n+\n+    stdout = result.stdout.strip()\n+    stderr = result.stderr.strip()\n+    if result.returncode not in (0, 1):\n+        logger.debug(\n+            \"Ruff failed for unused detection (code=%s): %s\",\n+            result.returncode,\n+            stderr or \"<no stderr>\",\n+        )\n+        return _UnusedToolAttempt(\n+            entries=[],\n+            status=\"failed\",\n+            error_kind=\"ruff_failed\",\n+            message=stderr or f\"ruff returned code {result.returncode}\",\n+        )\n+    if stderr and not stdout:\n+        logger.debug(\"Ruff reported stderr without JSON diagnostics: %s\", stderr)\n+        return _UnusedToolAttempt(\n+            entries=[],\n+            status=\"failed\",\n+            error_kind=\"ruff_stderr_only\",\n+            message=stderr,\n+        )\n+    if not stdout:\n+        if result.returncode == 0:\n+            return _UnusedToolAttempt(entries=[], status=\"clean\")\n+        logger.debug(\"Ruff returned nonzero without diagnostics.\")\n+        return _UnusedToolAttempt(\n+            entries=[],\n+            status=\"failed\",\n+            error_kind=\"ruff_no_diagnostics\",\n+            message=\"ruff returned nonzero without diagnostics\",\n+        )\n+\n+    try:\n+        diagnostics = json.loads(stdout)\n+    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:\n+        logger.debug(\"Ruff JSON parse failed: %s\", exc)\n+        return _UnusedToolAttempt(\n+            entries=[],\n+            status=\"failed\",\n+            error_kind=\"ruff_json_parse_failed\",\n+            message=str(exc),\n+        )\n+    if not isinstance(diagnostics, list):\n+        logger.debug(\n+            \"Ruff output had unexpected shape (%s).\",\n+            type(diagnostics).__name__,\n+        )\n+        return _UnusedToolAttempt(\n+            entries=[],\n+            status=\"failed\",\n+            error_kind=\"ruff_output_shape\",\n+            message=f\"unexpected output shape: {type(diagnostics).__name__}\",\n+        )\n+\n+    exclusions = _get_exclusions()\n+    entries = _parse_ruff_diagnostics(\n+        diagnostics, category=category, exclusions=exclusions\n+    )\n+    status: Literal[\"ok\", \"clean\"] = \"ok\" if entries else \"clean\"\n+    return _UnusedToolAttempt(entries=entries, status=status)\n+\n+\n+def _try_pyflakes(path: Path, category: str) -> _UnusedToolAttempt:\n+    \"\"\"Fallback: try pyflakes for unused detection.\"\"\"\n+    try:\n+        result = subprocess.run(\n+            [\"pyflakes\", str(path)],\n+            capture_output=True,\n+            text=True,\n+            cwd=PROJECT_ROOT,\n+            timeout=60,\n+        )\n+    except FileNotFoundError as exc:\n+        return _UnusedToolAttempt(\n+            entries=[],\n+            status=\"unavailable\",\n+            error_kind=\"pyflakes_not_found\",\n+            message=str(exc),\n+        )\n+    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as exc:\n+        return _UnusedToolAttempt(\n+            entries=[],\n+            status=\"failed\",\n+            error_kind=\"pyflakes_timeout\",\n+            message=str(exc),\n+        )\n+\n+    exclusions = _get_exclusions()\n+    lines = (result.stdout + result.stderr).splitlines()\n+    entries = _parse_pyflakes_lines(lines, category=category, exclusions=exclusions)\n+    status: Literal[\"ok\", \"clean\"] = \"ok\" if entries else \"clean\"\n+    return _UnusedToolAttempt(entries=entries, status=status)\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"UnusedDetectionResult\", \"detect_unused\", \"detect_unused_result\"]\n*** End Patch\n"
}
